= PAS-DAMMIM - PEOR
:toc:

== PAS-DAMMIM

* `1Ch 11:13` He was with David at *_Pas-dammim_*, and there the Philistines were gathered to battle, where a parcel of ground full of barley was, and the people fled from before the Philistines.

== PASACH

* `Psa 25:17` [*_PE_*] The troubles of my heart are enlarged. O bring me out of my distresses.
* `Psa 119:129` [*_PE_*] Your testimonies are wonderful; therefore, my soul keeps them.
* `Pro 31:26` [*_PE_*] She opens her mouth with wisdom, and on her tongue is the law of kindness.
* `Lam 1:17` [*_PE_*] Zion spreads forth her hands, and there is no one to comfort her. The LORD has commanded concerning Jacob, that his adversaries should be around him. Jerusalem has become an unclean thing among them.
* `Lam 2:17` [*_PE_*] The LORD has done that which he had devised. He has fulfilled his word that he had commanded in the days of old. He has thrown down and has not pitied. He has caused your enemy to rejoice over you. He has set up the horn of your adversaries.
* `Lam 3:49` [*_PE_*] My eye trickles down and does not cease without any intermission,
* `Lam 4:17` [*_PE_*] As for us, our eyes still fail, looking in vain for our help. In our watching we have watched for a nation that could not save us.
